Do you feel like you’re eating a larger amount of food than most people would in a short period of time, and do you try to compensate for it in any way, like vomiting or exercising?\n\nPatient: Yeah, it feels like a lot sometimes. Like I’ll skip meals or just forget to eat during the day, and then late at night I’m suddenly tearing through takeout and snacks after midnight. That’s honestly what scared me enough to come in. I haven’t really been keeping track of how much compared to other people, but it feels out of control when it happens.\n\nDoctor: When you're eating a lot late at night, do you feel like you can't stop eating or control how much you're eating?\n\nPatient: Yeah, in the moment it feels kind of hard to stop. It’s not every night, but when it happens, I’ll realize I’m just going through takeout and snacks after midnight, especially if I forgot to eat earlier, and it feels all over the place.\n\nDoctor: When you're eating a lot late at night, do you feel like you can't control how much you're eating or that you can't stop once you've started?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I guess that’s the part that worries me. Some days I’ll barely eat or forget meals completely, and then other nights I’m up after midnight just tearing through takeout and snacks. I haven’t really been keeping track of how much compared to other people, but it feels out of control when it happens.\n\nDoctor: When you're eating a lot late at night, do you feel like you can't stop eating or control how much you're eating?\n\nPatient: Yeah, in the moment it feels kind of hard to stop. Some days I’ll barely eat or forget meals completely, and then other nights I’m up after midnight just tearing through takeout and snacks.
